# 5.0.0 findings ledger

Every finding of the release audit of candidate `12a7562` (the 4.0.0 → 5.0.0 delta plus a full API–CLI–skill sweep), with its final disposition on the release tree. The release record is [v5.0.0.md](v5.0.0.md).

Each FIXED finding was reproduced with a failing regression test (or, for documentation, a failing doc assertion), fixed, and checked by a fresh independent reviewer on its PR. FIXED_LATER findings were closed by a later PR than the one first assigned. DUPLICATE findings point at the finding that covers them. DEFERRED findings are tracked in [`BACKLOG.md`](../../BACKLOG.md) with their release impact.

| Severity | FIXED   | FIXED_LATER | DUPLICATE | REJECTED | DEFERRED | OPEN_BLOCKER | Total   |
| -------- | ------- | ----------- | --------- | -------- | -------- | ------------ | ------- |
| CRITICAL | 0       | 1           | 0         | 0        | 0        | 0            | 1       |
| HIGH     | 47      | 2           | 7         | 0        | 0        | 0            | 56      |
| MEDIUM   | 127     | 5           | 4         | 1        | 1        | 0            | 138     |
| LOW      | 133     | 10          | 7         | 1        | 5        | 0            | 156     |
| **All**  | **307** | **18**      | **18**    | **2**    | **6**    | **0**        | **351** |

## Finding details

Reproduction, impact, proposed fix and test plan as recorded by the audit (abridged).

### F000

F1: `pages list` cannot send id/sort/subtype (silently ignored) and mis-serializes multi-value --status as a single CSV value

- Severity: HIGH; area: parity; disposition: FIXED; PR #378
- Location: src/cli/commands/confluence.ts:112-120; src/confluence/types/pages.ts:104-128; skill/reference/confluence.md:47,86
- Reproduction: Scratch copy, real runAtlas + fetch mock. `atlas confluence pages list --id 1,2 --sort=-title --subtype live --space-id 9` -> exit 0, request `GET /wiki/api/v2/pages?space-id=9` (id/sort/subtype dropped; --id/--sort/--subtype are registered global options so no Unknown option error, the handler just never reads them). `atlas confluence pages list --status current,archived` -> `GET /wiki/api/v2/pag…
- Impact: A model or user filtering pages by ID, sorting, restricting to live docs, or by two statuses gets either an unfiltered/unsorted listing (silently) or a wrong-wire request that the server treats as one nonexistent status. SDK ListPagesParams.id/sort/subtype/status[] have no CLI path, violating the CL…
- Fix: In the `pages list` case read `--id` (CSV, validated digits -> string[]), `--sort` (asEnum PAGE_SORT_ORDERS), `--subtype` (asEnum live\|page), `--status` via asEnumArray(['current','archived','deleted','trashed']), `--body-format` via asEnum(CONTENT_BODY_FORMATS). Update skill/reference/confluence.m…
- Test: Add a row to test/e2e/cli-confluence.test.ts using expectUrl: argv `pages list --id 1,2 --status current,archived --sort=-title --subtype live --space-id 9`, assert URL contains `id=1&id=2`, `status=current&status=archived`, `sort=-title`, `subtype=live`, `space-id=9` and not `%2C`; plus unit reject…
- Final evidence: test/cli/release-410-conf-a.test.ts 'F000: pages list forwards id/sort/subtype/status/body-format' (6 cases). Fails on origin/main src: id/sort/subtype dropped, status sent as one comma-joined value, bad enums sent anyway. Passes on branch.

### F001

F2: `pages update` hard-codes status=current and storage body; draft updates, ADF/wiki bodies and version message are unreachable and --body-json/--body-representation/--status are silently ignored

- Severity: HIGH; area: parity; disposition: FIXED; PR #378
- Location: src/cli/commands/confluence.ts:161-179 (status at :171, body at :175); src/confluence/types/pages.ts:218-244; test/cli/cli-write-property-reachability.test.ts:23-24 (key-presence-only analyzer)
- Reproduction: `atlas confluence pages update 1 --title T --version-number 1 --body '<p/>' --status draft --body-representation atlas_doc_format` -> exit 0, `PUT /wiki/api/v2/pages/1` body `{"id":"1","title":"T","status":"current","version":{"number":1},"body":{"representation":"storage","value":"<p/>"}}` (draft status and ADF representation dropped without error). `... --body '{"type":"doc"} --body-json '{}'` -…
- Impact: Pages authored in atlas_doc_format or wiki cannot be edited from the CLI without being corrupted to storage; drafts cannot be updated; a silent-ignore of an accepted-looking flag corrupts content rather than failing. Hard-rule CLI/skill parity violation (complex bodies must be reachable via JSON-val…
- Fix: Mirror `create`: add `--status current\|draft` (default current), `--body-representation storage\|atlas_doc_format\|wiki` (default storage), optional `--version-message`; reject `--body-json` explicitly on update or support the flat PageBodyWrite object. Allow empty `--body`. Document the new flags…
- Test: E2E row: `pages update 12345 --title T --version-number 1 --body '{"type":"doc"}' --body-representation atlas_doc_format --status draft --version-message m`, assert PUT body `{status:'draft', body:{representation:'atlas_doc_format',...}, version:{number:1,message:'m'}}`. Extend the reachability test…
- Final evidence: 'F001/F008: pages update sends status, representation and version message': draft ADF update with --message; current storage default with an empty body; trashed status, raw representation and --body-json rejected. Red on base, which hard-coded status=current and a storage body.

### F002

F3: `spaces create` cannot send roleAssignments (accepted KNOWN_GAP contradicts the CLI/skill parity hard rule)

- Severity: HIGH; area: parity; disposition: FIXED; PR #378
- Location: src/cli/commands/confluence.ts:492-514; src/confluence/types/spaces.ts:132-148; test/cli/cli-write-property-reachability.test.ts:78-81; skill/reference/confluence.md:167,186
- Reproduction: `atlas confluence spaces create --name N --key K --role-assignments '[]'` -> exit 1 `Unknown option '--role-assignments'`. Handler (:502-512) builds only name/key/alias/description/createPrivateSpace/templateKey/copySpaceAccessConfiguration. SDK CreateSpaceData.roleAssignments (spec POST /spaces property `roleAssignments`) is documented in the type as the way to create with an explicit grant set.…
- Impact: Space creation with an explicit RBAC grant set (the main non-private use of the endpoint on RBAC tenants) is impossible from the CLI; skill never tells the reader this. CLAUDE.md says 'hard rule, no exceptions' and requires JSON-valued flags for complex bodies, so the allowlist is a documented viola…
- Fix: Add `--role-assignments '<json array of {principal:{principalType,principalId},roleId}>'` (same parse/validation as `spaces set-role-assignments --value`), document it in the spaces `create` row and bullet L186, then delete the KNOWN_GAPS entry.
- Test: E2E row `spaces create --name N --key K --role-assignments '[{"principal":{"principalType":"USER","principalId":"a"},"roleId":"r"}]'` asserting POST /spaces body.roleAssignments; remove the KNOWN_GAPS entry so the existing reachability test (stale-entry check) enforces it.
- Final evidence: 'F002: spaces create sends roleAssignments from --value' (uses the already-registered --value, the same JSON array as set-role-assignments). The KNOWN_GAPS entry 'confluence.spaces.create' is removed from cli-write-property-reachability.test.ts. Red on base.

### F003

F4: `pages footer-comments` / `inline-comments` --status and --resolution-status accept ONE value and reject `archived`

- Severity: MEDIUM; area: cli; disposition: FIXED; PR #378
- Location: src/cli/commands/confluence.ts:362-391, COMMENT_STATUSES at :3204-3210; src/confluence/types/common.ts:143
- Reproduction: `atlas confluence pages footer-comments 1 --status archived` -> exit 1 `--status must be one of: current, deleted, trashed, historical, draft, got: archived`; `--status current,deleted` -> exit 1 (`got: current,deleted`); `pages inline-comments 1 --resolution-status open,dangling` -> exit 1. SDK `CommentStatus` includes `archived`, ListPage{Footer,Inline}CommentsParams.status and resolution-status…
- Impact: Archived comments and multi-status/multi-resolution filtering (e.g. open+reopened) cannot be requested from the CLI; SDK inputs unreachable.
- Fix: Add 'archived' to COMMENT_STATUSES (or a dedicated list matching the spec) and parse `--status` and `--resolution-status` with asEnumArray in both pages actions (and blog-posts).
- Test: E2E rows with expectUrl for `pages footer-comments 12345 --status current,archived` and `pages inline-comments 12345 --status current --resolution-status open,reopened` asserting repeated params; fails today with exit 1.
- Final evidence: 'F003/F014: comment listings accept multi-value status filters' › pages footer-comments/inline-comments send repeated status (incl. archived) and resolution-status. Red on base (asEnum single value, archived rejected).

### F004

F5: Skill documents wrong value sets for pages footer-comments/inline-comments filters

- Severity: MEDIUM; area: skill-docs; disposition: FIXED; PR #378
- Location: skill/reference/confluence.md:101-102 (also table rows 72-73)
- Reproduction: Line 101: '`--status` accepts `current` or `deleted`'; line 102: '`--resolution-status` (`open` or `resolved`)'. Actual CLI (probe): --status accepts current, deleted, trashed, historical, draft (archived rejected, see F4); --resolution-status accepts resolved, open, dangling, reopened. Spec: status enum current\|archived\|trashed\|deleted\|historical\|draft, resolution-status resolved\|open\|dang…
- Impact: A skill-only reader under-uses the filters (cannot discover dangling/reopened/trashed/draft) and gets wrong guidance about what is rejected.
- Fix: Document the full enum sets and (after F4) comma-separated multi-value support in both bullets and the blog-posts counterpart.
- Test: N/A for pure docs; after F4 the e2e rows prove the documented values. Optionally extend test/cli/skill-content.test.ts to assert documented enum tokens exist in the CLI allowlists.
- Final evidence: Docs: skill/reference/confluence.md pages footer-/inline-comments bullets and the blog-posts counterpart now list the full spec enums and the comma form. The example 'pages footer-comments 12345 --status current,archived' runs green in the examples test and red on base.

### F005

F6: `spaces list-properties --sort` sends a `sort` query param the pinned spec does not define for GET /spaces/{space-id}/properties

- Severity: MEDIUM; area: wire; disposition: FIXED; PR #378
- Location: src/confluence/resources/spaces.ts:530-547,554-568; src/cli/commands/confluence.ts:650-658; skill/reference/confluence.md:197; test/e2e/cli-confluence.test.ts:350-356; test/confluence/spaces.test.ts:866,1076
- Reproduction: `atlas confluence spaces list-properties 1 --key k --sort=-key --cursor c --limit 2` -> `GET /wiki/api/v2/spaces/1/properties?key=k&sort=-key&cursor=c&limit=2`. spec/confluence-v2.json GET /spaces/{space-id}/properties parameters = space-id, key, cursor, limit only (python dump); GET /pages/{page-id}/properties does define sort (key,-key). The e2e test (:350-356) and resource tests assert the undo…
- Impact: Wire call deviates from the contract (server may ignore silently or reject strictly); skill and tests advertise a feature the API does not have.
- Fix: Stop emitting `sort` for space properties (use a narrower params type without sort for SpacesResource, or at minimum drop it from the CLI case, the skill bullet L197 and the e2e assertion); keep sort for pages/blog-posts/etc. where the spec has it.
- Test: Change the e2e row to `spaces list-properties 654321 --key k` and assert no `sort` in the query; add a resource test that listProperties never emits `sort`. Fails against current code if sort is passed and asserted absent.
- Final evidence: SDK: release-410-conf-a-sdk.test.ts 'F005: SpacesResource never sends sort for space properties' (red on base). CLI: 'F005: spaces list-properties never sends sort' › rejects --sort. The e2e row and spaces.test.ts were updated; the spec has key, cursor and limit only.

It failed before with TS2554 'Expected 2-4 arguments, but got 1' and TS7006 in confluence.md, plus TS2304 'Cannot find name JiraClient' in jira.md. Now it passes. The confluence.md snippet uses client.pages.listAll({…

F8: No CLI->HTTP end-to-end coverage for the six 4.1.0-new pages restriction/permission actions, comment listings, or filtered list/get; reachability analyzer is key-presence-only

Enumerating argv in test/e2e/cli-confluence.test.ts for resources pages/spaces shows no rows for pages get-restriction-state, set-restriction-state, list-permissions, add-permissions, set-permissions, delete-permissions, footer-comments, inline-comments, and none that exercise filtered `pages list`/`spac…

- Final evidence: 'F007/F024/F036: wire coverage…' › pages and blog-posts restriction-state/permissions actions, asserting method, path, query and body end to end. Coverage gap only: the wire was already correct on base.

### F008

F9: `update-property` cannot send version.message (pages and spaces; also pages update)

- Severity: LOW; area: parity; disposition: FIXED; PR #378

- Reproduction: `atlas confluence pages update-property 1 --property-id p --key k --value 1 --version-number 3` -> PUT body `{"key":"k","value":1,"version":{"number":3}}`; there is no flag for the optional `version.message` that UpdateSharedContentPropertyData and the spec ContentPropertyUpdateRequest (version.message: 'Message to be associated with the new version') define. - Test: E2E rows asserting body.version.message for pages and spaces update-property.

Parts 1-2 are still open: types/pages.ts:113 status is still the wide PageContentStatus (draft/historical/any), but spec GET /pages status only allows current\|archived\|deleted\|trashed; types/pages.ts:107 spaceId is still a single string (sp…

SDK inputs params.id and multi-valued param…

- Final evidence: 'F011: blog-posts list forwards id and multi-value status' (repeated id/status, no %2C; bogus status rejected). Red on base.

### F012

blog-posts create/update cannot send draft status, createdAt, spaceId, version.message; unsupported flags silently ignored

- Severity: MEDIUM; area: parity; disposition: FIXED; PR #378
- Location: src/cli/commands/confluence.ts:719-741; src/confluence/types/blog-posts.ts:83-117
- Reproduction: `blog-posts create --space-id 1 --title T --status draft` => exit 0, POST body {spaceId,title} (no status: server publishes a non-draft). `blog-posts update 9 --title T --version-number 2 --status draft --body x` => body status:'current'. `--body-representation wiki` is dropped (representation fixed storage; spec says blog create supports storage only, so only status/createdAt/spaceId/version.mess…
- Impact: A user cannot create a draft or demote a post to draft from the CLI (spec status enum current\|draft, title optional for drafts) and a plausible `--status draft` publishes content instead; pages create supports --status, so behaviour is inconsistent across the five content types.
- Fix: Add --status (asEnum current\|draft) to create and update (relax --title for drafts), optional --created-at; reject registered-but-unsupported flags (--body-representation) with an error; document in confluence.md L243-244.
- Test: commands.test.ts + e2e: `create --status draft` expects POST body.status==='draft'; `update --status draft` expects body.status==='draft'; `--body-representation wiki` on blog-posts expects an error.
- Final evidence: 'F012/F013: blog-posts create/update/delete': draft without title, private back-dated wiki post, update to draft with --message/--created-at, --body-json rejected. The pinned spec (createBlogPost/updateBlogPost body oneOf storage\|atlas_doc_format\|wiki) contradicts the finding's 'storage only' premise, so --body-representation is supported instead of rejected. Red on base.

- Fix: Use asEnum(opts['body-format'], CONTENT_BODY_FORMATS, 'body-format') in list and get.
- Test: commands.test.ts: tasks list/get with bogus --body-format throws (fails before).
- Final evidence: release-410-conf-b '["tasks","list"] --body-format bogus is rejected' and '["tasks","get","123456"] ...'. Both fail on base, which sent body-format=bogus.

### F051

tasks.listAll and versions.listAllForPage skip client-side limit validation

- Final evidence: test/confluence/tasks.test.ts 'listAll() throws ValidationError for limit 0\|-1\|1.5 before any request (F051)' and test/confluence/versions.test.ts 'listAllForPage() ... (F051)'. Both fail on base.

### F057

The systemic case (any unsupported global flag is dropped) is in notesForOrchestrator.

Spec also says principal-id requires principal-type and vice versa, and lists valid ACCESS_CLASS ids (anonymous-users, jsm-project-admins, authenticated-users, all-licensed-users, all-product-admins); the skill mentions none of this.
- Impact: A tenant-defined role type cannot be filtered from the CLI, and an agent constructing `--principal-type USER` alone from the skill gets a server 400.
- Fix: Either loosen --role-type to a free string (keeping SYSTEM\|CUSTOM as documented examples) or state the restriction as deliberate in the SDK JSDoc; add the pairing rule and the ACCESS_CLASS principal-id values to confluence.md:814.
- Test: If loosened: commands.test.ts asserts --role-type X passes through. Docs part N/A.
- Final evidence: Round-2 minor fix. `space-roles list --role-type ""` or `" "` now exits 1 with '--role-type must not be empty' and sends no request, via nonEmptyFilter. Before this fix it sent `role-type=`; base had rejected it through asEnum. Test: 'rejects an empty --role-type %j instead of sending role-type=' (red, then green). Documented in the skill.

### F092

projects get: --expand silently ignored (SDK expand param unreachable) and SDK lacks the spec's `properties` query

- Severity: MEDIUM; area: parity; disposition: FIXED; PR #381
- Location: src/cli/commands/jira.ts:766-767; src/jira/resources/projects.ts:378-389; skill/reference/jira/projects.md:49
- Reproduction: `atlas jira projects get PROJ --expand description,lead` => exit 0, `GET /rest/api/3/project/PROJ` with no query (flag registered globally, never read by the `get` case). SDK `get(projectIdOrKey, expand?: string[])` therefore has an unreachable input. Spec GET /project/{key} also defines `properties` (array<string>; 'only the properties named here are returned in properties, empty when omitted') w…
- Impact: Users cannot request expansions (issueTypes, lead, projectKeys, insight...) or project properties from the CLI; the silent no-op looks like success. Skill row documents no flags, so a user cannot discover this either.
- Fix: CLI: pass `csvFlag(opts['expand'])` and a new `--properties` CSV; SDK: add optional second-argument/params-object form (backward compatible overload) that appends `properties` as repeated params. Document both in projects.md L49.
- Test: runAtlas e2e asserting `GET /project/PROJ?expand=description%2Clead&properties=a&properties=b`; unit test for the new SDK overload.
- Final evidence: test/cli/release-410-jira-projects.test.ts > projects get (F092) > 'sends --expand as CSV and --properties as repeated params'; test/jira/projects.test.ts > get() > 'accepts a params object: expand as CSV, properties as repeated params'. Both red on base. Spec: expand is type:string (CSV) and properties is type:array (repeated).

### F093

projects get-role-details: excludeOtherServiceRoles cannot be passed from the CLI

- Final evidence: test/cli/release-410-jira-projects.test.ts > get-role-details --exclude-other-service-roles (F093) > 'sends true, explicit false, and nothing when omitted'. Red on base, where the flag was an Unknown option.

### F096

### F097

### F098

Skill: projects delete --enable-undo is listed as a bare flag but the router registers it as a value flag (default true)

- Impact: A skill-following agent hitting the documented flag gets a parse error; permanent deletion (the only non-default use) is undiscoverable.

- Test: N/A (docs); add a router.test.ts case for `--enable-undo false`.
- Final evidence: Docs fix: the skill now documents --enable-undo true\|false (default true = recycle bin) with a permanent-delete example. It is pinned by test/cli/release-410-jira-projects.test.ts > 'delete sends --enable-undo true\|false and nothing when omitted' (also checks that a bare flag exits 1). The code was already correct, so this test passes on base too.

### F103

add-role-actors drops unrecognised --body keys; restore discards the returned Project

- Severity: LOW; area: cli; disposition: FIXED; PR #381
- Location: src/jira/resources/projects.ts:813-828; src/cli/commands/jira.ts:963-965,988-999
- Reproduction: `atlas jira projects add-role-actors PROJ 10001 --body '{"users":["a"]}'` (typo) => POST body {} exit 0. `atlas jira projects restore PROJ` prints {"restored":true} though the SDK returns the restored Project (spec 200 Project).
- Impact: Typos in the JSON body produce silent no-op requests; restored project data is thrown away.
- Fix: Validate that --body contains at least one of user/group/groupId; print the Project for restore.

### F104

Role ID interpolated into the URL path without validation or encoding in four ProjectsResource methods

- Severity: LOW; area: security; disposition: FIXED; PR #381
- Location: src/jira/resources/projects.ts:748,765,824,838
- Reproduction: Scratch probe b7-roleid.test.ts: `client.projects.getRole('PROJ', '1/../../../../myself?x=' as unknown as number)` produces path `https://x.atlassian.net/rest/api/3/project/PROJ/role/1/../../../../myself?x=`. The CLI is safe (asPositiveInt) and TypeScript types it as number, but JS callers/casts are not protected; 99 other sites use encodePathSegment(String(id)).
- Impact: Defense-in-depth gap: untyped callers can steer a request to another endpoint (same class as the B1052 fix). Not reachable via the CLI.
- Fix: Validate with Number.isSafeInteger and use encodePathSegment(String(roleId)) in deleteRoleActors, getRole, addRoleActors, setRoleActors.
- Test: Unit tests passing a traversal string and a non-integer, expecting ValidationError.
- Final evidence: test/jira/projects.test.ts > role ID validation > getRole/deleteRoleActors/addRoleActors/setRoleActors 'rejects a traversal, non-integer, or non-positive roleId' (4 cases). Red on base, where the traversal string reached the path. The fix uses roleIdSegment(): Number.isSafeInteger plus encodePathSegment.

### F114

The async 303 task is documented in metadata.md and the JSDoc; the transport follows the 303 as a GET. Returning the task payload is not done (see notes)

### F168

Same code in v4.0.0 (not a regression).

- Test: runAtlas e2e asserting ?status=PUBLISHED&status=DRAFT&orderBy=rank; fails before.
- Final evidence: Round 3: classification-levels --status sends each status once (spec uniqueItems).

Spec: add/delete 'cannot add/delete groups and users in the same request'; POST /role/{id}: 'cannot update both... if both sent only…

### F211

CLI discards results of async/returning mutations: resolutions delete (TaskProgress) and roles delete-actors (Role)

- Severity: LOW; area: cli; disposition: FIXED; PR #380

Likewise `await client.roles.deleteActors(...)` returns the updated Role but CLI prints {deleted:true}.
- Impact: User cannot poll the deletion task or see remaining actors from the delete call; skill never says resolution deletion is asynchronous. The resource comment still claims the CLI passes replaceWith conditionally, which is no longer true.
- Fix: Return the SDK result for these two actions (or at least the task id) and document the async nature and `task get` polling; refresh the stale comment.
- Test: commands.test.ts: assert returned value equals mocked task/role.
- Final evidence: 'resolutions > delete prints the deletion task Jira returns (F211)' (DELETE 303 -> GET /task/10050, stdout = task) and 'roles > delete-actors prints the role Jira returns (F211)'. Stale DEFERRED-CLI comment removed.

### F232

Skill's primary project-template create example sends project.projectType; spec field is projectTypeKey (ProjectPayload additionalProperties:false)

- Reproduction: Replayed the skill command through runCli: exit 0, wire body details{...}, template {"project":{"projectType":"software"}}. Spec ProjectPayload has additionalProperties:false and only projectTypeKey (enum software\|business\|service_desk\|product_discovery) plus *SchemeId identifiers; `projectType` is not a property.
- Impact: The one documented create example submits an invalid capability payload that Jira will reject (or ignore, creating no usable project); an agent copying it fails. skill-content.test only parse-validates SKILL.md/examples.md, not projects.md, and --template is an untyped Record so tsc cannot catch it.
- Fix: Change example to {"project":{"projectTypeKey":"software"}} (and add a minimal valid example for workflow/issueType capabilities); fix the fixtures in test/jira/project-template.test.ts; optionally add a spec-driven check that --template example keys exist in CustomTemplateRequestDTO/ProjectPayload.
- Test: Test that extracts the --template JSON from projects.md and validates top-level and project.* keys against the pinned spec schemas; fails before (projectType), passes after.
- Final evidence: 'skill --template example only uses keys the spec defines (F232)'. The fixtures in test/jira/project-template.test.ts and test/cli/commands.test.ts now use projectTypeKey, matching ProjectPayload, which is additionalProperties:false and defines only projectTypeKey.

### F266

computeQsh hashes a relative path as-is, so direct transport.request callers with relative paths on Confluence Connect get a wrong QSH

- Severity: LOW; area: docs; disposition: FIXED_LATER
- Location: src/core/connect-jwt.ts:84-86, src/core/connect-jwt.ts:179-188
- Reproduction: minor-repro.test.ts: computeQsh('GET', '/pages', undefined, '/wiki') equals sha256('GET&/pages&'). The request actually sent (baseUrl https://x/wiki/api/v2 plus /pages) needs canonical '/api/v2/pages', so this prints `rel ok= false`. The absolute form passes (`abs ok= true`). A grep found that every library resource sends absolute `${this.baseUrl}/...` paths, so only direct transport.request users…
- Impact: Connect apps that call transport.request({path:'/pages'}) directly get a 401 that gives no hint about the cause. This is documented only in the computeQsh JSDoc, not in the README Connect section.
- Fix: Add a sentence to the README Connect section (around README.md:383-385): when using createConnectJwtMiddleware with transport.request, pass an absolute URL, because relative paths are hashed as-is. Or have the middleware resolve relative paths against the resolved baseUrl.
- Test: N/A (docs). If the code path is changed, test that a relative-path request through ConfluenceClient's transport with contextPath '/wiki' signs canonical '/api/v2/pages'.
- Final evidence: JSDoc connect-jwt.ts:42-45 and :91-93 (#373), plus a README sentence at :414 (#385): 'A relative path such as '/pages' is hashed as-is ... Atlassian rejects the token with a 401'. There is no code fix by design, because the middleware never sees the resolved baseUrl, and every resource emits absolute URLs.

### F303

README rate-limiter ordering guidance says 'after (outside) cache/batch', which contradicts the library's array-order semantics

- Severity: LOW; area: docs; disposition: FIXED; PR #371
- Location: README.md:523, docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:384
- Reproduction: ARCHITECTURE.md:384 says middleware is composed with reduceRight, 'first entry is the outermost wrapper'. For cache hits to avoid burning a token, the limiter must sit after cache in the array, which makes it inner. README:523 says 'after (outside) cache/batch so cached/deduped hits don't burn a token'. 'after' is correct; '(outside)' is wrong.
- Impact: A user who places the limiter outside cache/batch spends a token on every cached or deduplicated hit.
- Fix: Change to 'after (inside) cache/batch', and give an explicit example array `[breaker, oauth, cache, batch, rateLimiter]`.
- Test: N/A (docs).
- Final evidence: 'F303:' tests: README says 'after (inside) cache/batch' with [breaker, oauth, cache, batch, rateLimiter]; runtime proof via createMiddlewareChain: [cache, limiter] serves the cache hit with no token, [limiter, cache] throws RateLimiterExhaustedError.

- Deferred: Declare @types/node as an optional peer dependency (peerDependenciesMeta.optional), or document in README that consumers need Node types. Impact: consumers compiling with types:[] and skipLibCheck:false get TS2307 on node:crypto in dist/core/connect-jwt.d.ts. LOW.

### F343

payload-rules.md claims Jira issues come out as the issue key under --format minimal; they come out as the numeric id

- Severity: MEDIUM; area: skill-docs; disposition: FIXED; PR #374
- Location: skill/reference/payload-rules.md:11; src/cli/output.ts:238-245; skill/SKILL.md:50
- Reproduction: Scratch run.test.ts: `atlas jira issues get PROJ-123 --fields summary,status,assignee --format minimal` with mock {id:'10123',key:'PROJ-123',...} prints "10123\n". extractId prefers `id` (string or number) over `key`, and Jira issue JSON always carries a string `id`.
- Impact: payload-rules.md:11 says this yields the issue key (e.g. PROJ-123). Agents that pipe minimal output into commands or human-facing replies expecting keys get numeric ids. Ids are accepted by most issue endpoints, but a reply such as 'created PROJ-124' or key-based JQL (`key = 10123`) goes wrong.
- Fix: Change the doc to say Jira issues yield the numeric issue id and that `--format json` (`.key`) gives the key. Alternatively special-case objects with both `key` and `fields` to print `key`, but that changes minimal output for existing scripts, so the doc fix is preferred.
- Test: N/A for the doc fix. For the code option, an output.test.ts case printOutput({id:'1',key:'P-1',fields:{}}, 'minimal') expects 'P-1'.
- Final evidence: skill/reference/jira.md:136 now says `issues get --format minimal` prints the numeric issue ID, not the key. payload-rules.md and SKILL.md were already fixed.

The skill docs do cover them. The two behavior changes in this release (jira users create now requires --products; a top-level Confluence inline comment now requires --text-sel…

- Fix: Add a few JIRA_HELP and CONFLUENCE_HELP EXAMPLES for the #365 write flags (transition --resolution, create --parent, users create --products, comments create --text-selection ...). Change help.e2e to compare the comma-split action set against the dispatcher in both directions.
- Test: Replace toContain with a set comparison in help.e2e.test.ts. To check it, add an extra action name to a help line in a scratch copy: the strengthened test must fail where the current one passes.
- Final evidence: The write-flag examples (see F278) are in, plus a PER-ACTION FLAGS pointer in the global, Confluence and Jira help. test/cli/help.e2e.test.ts now compares help action lists with the dispatcher as exact sets. Proof: after injecting 'labels list, list-stale', the old test passes and the new one fails. The per-action `atlas <api> <resource> --help` flag tables are deferred: they need a src/cli/index.…

### F350

`install-skill --dry-run` reports success when the real install would exit 2 (the 4.0.0 → 4.1.0 upgrade case)

- Severity: LOW; area: cli; disposition: FIXED; PR #387
- Location: src/skill-installer/index.ts:397,417-425; skill/SKILL.md:58-67
- Reproduction: Scratch dry.test.ts creates a target whose SKILL.md says `version: 4.0.0`, then calls executeInstallSkill: - `{path: target, 'dry-run': true}`: exit 0, 'Would install 14 files to ...'. - `{path: target}`: exit 2, 'Target ... already exists with a different version (4.0.0). Pass --force to overwrite.' The version check is skipped under dryRun (`if (!options.dryRun && fs.exists(destSkillPath))`).
- Impact: Everyone with the 4.0.0 skill installed needs `--force` to upgrade to 4.1.0, and dry-run doesn't warn them. SKILL.md's install section doesn't mention the upgrade path. The e2e test checks only that files exist, not that their content matches.
- Fix: In dry-run mode, read the existing version and report noop or a conflict that needs --force (exit 0 with a warning, or exit 2). Add one SKILL.md line: 'upgrading from an older installed version: add --force'.
- Test: A test in test/skill-installer/install-skill.test.ts: an existing different-version target with dryRun returns a conflict or warning result. It fails today because the result is 'dry-run'.
- Final evidence: Round 2: the exit-3 (permission) exception is now stated in the changelog and the runInstall comment, and pinned by a test (real install exit 3, dry run 'dry-run').
